{\n533\t\terr := &ParseError{\n534\t\t\tInput: \"bad input\",\n535\t\t\tPhase: \"ldh\",\n536\t\t\tErr:   \"starts with hyphen\",\n537\t\t}\n538\t\tassert.Equal(t, `domain \"bad input\": ldh (starts with hyphen)`, err.Error())\n539\t}\n540\t\n541\t// FuzzValidateDomain fuzzes domain validation with random inputs.\n542\t// Properties verified:\n543\t//   - Never panics on any input\n544\t//   - Valid outputs always contain at least one dot (eTLD+1)\n545\t//   - Valid outputs have labels ≤63 characters\n546\t//   - Valid outputs have total length ≤253 characters\n547\tfunc FuzzValidateDomain(f *testing.F) {\n548\t\t// Seed corpus: 9 diverse inputs covering valid and invalid cases\n549\t\tseeds := []string{\n550\t\t\t\"example.com\",           // #1: Simple valid domain\n551\t\t\t\"EXAMPLE.COM\",           // #2: Uppercase (normalizes)\n552\t\t\t\"www.example.co.uk\",     // #3: Multi-level TLD with subdomain\n553\t\t\t\"münchen.de\",            // #4: IDN input (converts to punycode)\n554\t\t\t\"\",                      // #5: Empty string\n555\t\t\t\"example..com\",          // #6: Empty label\n556\t\t\t\"-example.com\",          // #7: Leading hyphen\n557\t\t\t\"http://example.com\",    // #8: URL scheme\n558\t\t\tstrings.Repeat(\"a\", 64) + \".com\", // #9: Label too long (64 chars)\n559\t\t}\n560\t\tfor _, seed := range seeds {\n561\t\t\tf.Add(seed)\n562\t\t}\n563\t\n564\t\tf.Fuzz(func(t *testing.T, input string) {\n565\t\t\t// Property 1: Must never panic (the test framework handles this)\n566\t\t\tresult, err := Parse(input)\n567\t\n568\t\t\t// If parsing succeeded, verify invariants\n569\t\t\tif err == nil && result != nil {\n570\t\t\t\t// Property 2: Valid domains contain at least one dot\n571\t\t\t\tif !strings.Contains(result.Domain, \".\") {\n572\t\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"valid domain missing dot: %q\", result.Domain)\n573\t\t\t\t}\n574\t\n575\t\t\t\t// Property 3: Each label ≤63 characters\n576\t\t\t\tlabels := strings.Split(result.Domain, \".\")\n577\t\t\t\tfor i, label := range labels {\n578\t\t\t\t\tif len(label) > 63 {\n579\t\t\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"label %d exceeds 63 chars (%d): %q\", i+1, len(label), label)\n580\t\t\t\t\t}\n581\t\t\t\t}\n582\t\n583\t\t\t\t// Property 4: Total domain length ≤253 characters\n584\t\t\t\tif len(result.Domain) > 253 {\n585\t\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"domain exceeds 253 chars (%d): %q\", len(result.Domain), result.Domain)\n586\t\t\t\t}\n587\t\t\t}\n588\t\t})\n589\t}\n590\t\n591\tfunc TestParse_EffectiveTLDPlusOne(t \"example.org\"},\n600\t\t\t{name: \"com.au\", input: \"www.example.com.au\", want: \"example.com.au\"},\n601\t\t\t{name: \"gov.uk\", input: \"www.direct.gov.uk\", want: {\n\terr := &ParseError{\n\t\tInput: \"bad input\",\n\t\tPhase: \"ldh\",\n\t\tErr:   \"starts with hyphen\",\n\t}\n\tassert.Equal(t, `domain \"bad input\": ldh (starts with hyphen)`, err.Error())\n}\n\n// FuzzValidateDomain fuzzes domain validation with random inputs.\n// Properties verified:\n//   - Never panics on any input\n//   - Valid outputs always contain at least one dot (eTLD+1)\n//   - Valid outputs have labels ≤63 characters\n//   - Valid outputs have total length ≤253 characters\nfunc FuzzValidateDomain(f *testing.F) {\n\t// Seed corpus: 9 diverse inputs covering valid and invalid cases\n\tseeds := []string{\n\t\t\"example.com\",           // #1: Simple valid domain\n\t\t\"EXAMPLE.COM\",           // #2: Uppercase (normalizes)\n\t\t\"www.example.co.uk\",     // #3: Multi-level TLD with subdomain\n\t\t\"münchen.de\",            // #4: IDN input (converts to punycode)\n\t\t\"\",                      // #5: Empty string\n\t\t\"example..com\",          // #6: the full Check flow with a mock server.\nfunc TestRDAPClientCheck(t *testing.T) {\n\tctx := context.Background()\n\n\tt.Run(\"registered domain\", func(t *testing.T) {\n\t\tserver := httptest.NewServer(http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\t\tif !strings.HasSuffix(r.URL.Path, \"/domain/example.com\") {\n\t\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"unexpected path: %s\", r.URL.Path)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/rdap+json\")\n\t\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)\n\t\t\t_, _ = w.Write([]byte(`{\n\t\t\t\t\"objectClassName\": \"domain\",\n\t\t\t\t\"ldhName\": \"example.com\",\n\t\t\t\t\"entities\": [{\"ldhName\": \"Test Registrar\", \"roles\": [\"registrar\"]}],\n\t\t\t\t\"events\": [\n\t\t\t\t\t{\"eventAction\": \"registration\", \"eventDate\": \"2020-01-01T00:00:00Z\"},\n\t\t\t\t\t{\"eventAction\": \"expiration\", \"eventDate\": \"2025-01-01T00:00:00Z\"}\n\t\t\t\t]\n\t\t\t}`))\n\t\t}))\n\t\tdefer server.Close()\n\n\t\tclient := newTestRDAPClient(server)\n\t\tresult, err := client.Check(ctx, \"example.com\")\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tt.Fatalf(\"Check failed: %v\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif result.Available {\n\t\t\tt.Error(\"expected domain to be registered\")\n\t\t}\n\t\tif result.Registration == nil || result.Registration.Registrar != \"Test Registrar\" {\n\t\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected
